Stage B banked **3 of 4** — `m2_region_00001`, `m2_region_00017`, `m2_region_00040__sub14`, all `ok`; bucket `.npz` count 3 — then `--fail-fast` halted on `m2_region_00057`. STEP 9-GATE deliberately **not run** (it would score an incomplete, error-bearing batch). `m2_region_00071` never ran → the worst-case cost anchor is missing (**COST-1**).

**What was MEASURED.** Region 00057's square LD carries **exactly 2 NaN cells of 8,145,316** — one symmetric pair at rows/cols **2532+2533**, `nan_count 1` each, **both diagonals 1.0**. The producer's error text blames "a zero-variance variant"; that is **falsified** — a zero-variance variant NaNs its entire row *and column* (~2,854 each), and both these variants have well-defined marginal variance. This is a **pairwise**-undefined correlation. The pair: the 1 bp deletion `chr15:20394741:AT:A` (`ref_len 2` → `span_end 20394742`) and the SNP `chr15:20394743:T:C` — **exactly one base past the span end**, so the occlusion filter (`d.pos < v.pos <= d.span_end`) behaved **correctly** and the pair is still undefined.

**MECHANISM CONFIRMED (23:38 EDT).** The joint-callability table totals **73,122** — the full cohort — and shows **perfect confounding: 0 of 871 deletion carriers are called at the flanking SNP** (the `('1','0')` and `('1','1')` cells are absent entirely). So within the **71,048** samples called at both, A is constant at 0 → zero variance → `0/0` → NaN, while A's **marginal** allele frequency is a healthy **0.601%** and B stays variable in that same intersection. The producer's message was right that zero variance caused it and **wrong about the scope** — intersection-invariant, not analysis-set-monomorphic — so its prescribed remedy would never have flagged A. **This is the occlusion mechanism, one base beyond the tested REF span.**

**⚠ Second-order consequence.** The NaN raise catches only the **perfect-confounding knife-edge**. *Partial* confounding leaves the intersection variable, plink returns a **finite** `r` computed on a biased subsample that under-represents carriers, and **nothing in the pipeline fires**. So region 1 passing NaN-free establishes only that it held no *perfectly* confounded pair — **not** that it is free of deletion-linked missingness bias. The Stage A falsification stands exactly as worded and says nothing about defined-but-biased `r`. The sweep must measure the **distribution** of confounding, not just count NaN.

**The negative control we already own.** Region 1 had **7,951** multi-base-REF rows, 231 occluded, and after exclusion the verifier re-read **38,595,391,746 B** with **zero NaN**. So deletion-boundary adjacency is **not** generically NaN-producing — 00057 is conditional on something further (hypothesis, *unmeasured*: the pairwise-complete intersection contains only non-carriers, leaving the deletion invariant within it).

**⛔ What must not happen.** Do **not** calibrate any criterion, threshold or policy on this one region — that is exactly the error that produced the withdrawn `0.0005` constant (n=1, wrong by ~38×). A 1 bp span widening is another **proxy** patch with an unmeasured cutoff; the detectable **property** is "the pairwise-complete intersection is invariant". And `--fail-fast` must not be carried into an ~11-day Stage C at an unknown per-region failure rate. **Stage C is not discussable until the prevalence number exists.**

**RAM-1 — confirmed defect (separate).** `_run_plink` computes `peak_kib = max(rss_after − rss_before, rss_after)`; `rss_before ≥ 0` so the delta is always ≤ `rss_after` and **the `max()` collapses to `rss_after` every time**, while `RUSAGE_CHILDREN.ru_maxrss` is a **monotone high-water mark** across all children in the process (verified empirically). So `peak_ram_gib` is "largest plink child so far in this invocation", not per-region: 00057 reports **26.5745 inherited from sub14** (a 2,854-variant region cannot use 26.6 GiB). Over 276 serial regions the column flat-lines. Clean fix: `Popen` + `os.wait4()`. Off the LD path; must land before Stage C.

**NEXT, in order.** (1) Confirm the mechanism on the banked pair — one read-only 2-variant `--recode A` joint-callability dump (seconds). (2) **Measure the prevalence** across the pre-committed 21-region sample with a *designed* probe (candidate pairs are geometric, and `r` for a pair is independent of the other variants → a candidate-only `--r` answers it in minutes, no full-region LD recompute). (3) Only then adjudicate criterion/policy (Seth, brief-blind) and Stage C's error posture. (4) Independently: RAM-1 and the 00071 anchor. Record: `.planning/debug/260824-STAGE-B-HALT-region57-boundary-adjacent-pairwise-NaN.md`. **AN AGENT NEVER FIRES.**

**The gate, exactly as posted.** A region DEFERS when **EITHER** (i) its occluded-**SITE** fraction `occ_sites / n_sites` **strictly exceeds** `0.005056` (0.5056% = 3× the 21-region site-basis median 0.1685%) **OR** (ii) its **own** row/site inflation at occluded sites `occ_rows / occ_sites` **strictly exceeds** `3.42` (3× the inflation median 1.14x). Strict `>` on both, so equality on either condition stays on the exclude-in-lockstep path. **Accounting did not move to sites:** `_PANEL_COLUMNS` is byte-unchanged at 9 and `n_dropped_occluded` is still a ROW count. **Both routes emit the same `deferred_occlusion_anomaly:` prefix**, so the ast status-vocabulary enforcer needed no fourth branch. Shipping the ceiling alone would have been a scope reduction the posted record does not authorise; it was not done.

**The false premise is gone, not merely superseded.** `grep -rn` for the withdrawn single-condition global over `src/` and `tests/` returns **zero hits** (the test that bans it assembles the name from two pieces so it does not defeat its own grep), and a banned-literals test scans the **comment-stripped** source of **both** consumers for `0.0005` / `0.005056` / `0.5056` / `3.42`. That scan treats a **docstring or f-string as CODE**, which is why `fire_verifier`'s two remaining false-premise prose sites — the `check_manifest_rows` docstring ("the ONLY region with a known answer") and the `check_region1_status` detail ("~51 ceiling, 10x headroom") — now **render** the ceilings from the accessors instead of quoting them. Those two sentences were the last live statements of the false premise in shipped code and the float-only ban could never have caught them.

**NEW CONTRACT, deliberate and recorded as a contract change rather than an edit-to-green:** a **deferred** region now ships exactly **ONE** object to the bucket — its `{region_id}.occlusion_gate.json`. The predecessor test asserted the blanket "no region-1 object crosses"; that is replaced by an **exact one-object allow-list** plus explicit negatives for `.npz` / `.afreq` / `.occluded.excludelist` / `.occlusion_manifest.tsv`. The `verify_failed` path still crosses nothing but the panel row. The sidecar write on a deferring region is **deliberately not** wrapped in the manifest's best-effort `try/except`: there, it is the only evidence that outlives the region, so an unwritable sidecar must surface as `error:` rather than become a silent deferral.

**Why T2 is one commit and not two.** `_default_occlusion_fraction()` read the producer global at CALL time, so deleting that global in a producer-only commit would have left four `test_fire_verifier` tests RED between two commits. A green that follows a planned red carries less information than a green that never broke, so the verifier surface was merged in. **`tests/m3` is 0 failed at every commit in this batch** (T2: 1014P/32S; T3: 1021P/33S; T4: docs only).

**REGION 1 EXPECT — MEASURED 2026-08-19/20, now in every runbook.** `status "ok"`, **`n_dropped_occluded == 231`** (231 occluded ROWS at **196** SITES, of **96,708** sites / 102,421 rows → **0.2027%** site fraction and **1.18x** inflation, **under BOTH** ceilings); manifest **232 lines (header + 231 records)**; sidecar `occ_rows 231, occ_sites 196, n_sites 96708, fired [], verdict ok`. **THE ARBITER RULE:** the manifest line count, the panel row and the sidecar are three records of ONE drop set — on any disagreement the **sidecar is the arbiter** and the run **STOPS for re-measurement**. Never edit-to-green, never "close enough".

**The oracle no longer claims more than was measured.** Probe 2 (2026-08-19) found **231** occluded variants over **7,951** multi-base-REF rows with the settled 5 a strict **subset**, so the two `==` assertions were *false about the window* while true only about the June-2026 NaN-pair forensics. LAYER 1 now asserts **containment** on both halves (written in the version-independent per-key form, because `Counter.__le__` is multiset containment only on py3.10+ and that test **runs** in the perimeter). `_REGION1_REAL_SAME_POSITION_COUNT` is re-scoped to `_REGION1_NAN_PAIRS_SAME_POSITION_COUNT` so the lost scope travels in the name, and is never asserted window-wide. LAYER 2 is a **separate** gated test whose name and docstring both carry `MEASURED_NOT_DERIVED`, pinning `n_rows 102421 / n_deletion_rows 7951 / n_occluded_rows 231 / max_span 170 / n_sites 96708 / occ_sites 196` with full provenance and the rule **"on a break: RE-MEASURE AND RECORD, never edit-to-green"**. A new **unconditional** control runs both containment shapes against wrong inputs, because the gated test skips outside the perimeter and can never demonstrate its own failability.

**Stage A stopped taking a human's number.** `check_manifest_rows` lost its `expected_records` **default** (the silent fallback that carried "5" into the fire runbook); `_stage_a` now DERIVES the count from the region's `.occluded.excludelist` line count and CROSS-CHECKS it against the sidecar's `occ_rows`, failing closed on absent / unreadable / mismatch, and emitting it as its own `expected_records_derivation` check. `--expected-records` survives only as an override and is **logged as one**, next to the derived value it displaced. The 230-vs-231 mismatch control differs from the green case in exactly one field and asserts on the detail text, not just the exit code.

**★ THE SINGLE MOST CONSEQUENTIAL FINDING — Track-A EUR numerics were moving, silently.**
BLOCKER-1 had been *silently pinning* every EUR fit to the 1kG tail, because `run_susie_rss.R`
always rebuilt its own path. m3-04c's `--ld-file` thread removed that pin **for every ancestry, not
just AFR**. **PROVEN:** EUR `r[1,2]` 0.1 → 0.9, credible sets 3 → 10, nonzero PIPs 200 → 78 — while
`ld_status` **and** `ld_overlap_fraction`, *the two fields anyone would check to argue nothing
moved*, stayed **BYTE-IDENTICAL**. Track A is in submission. **NOW CLOSED END-TO-END** by a config
allow-list (`ld_read_path.ancestries`, mirroring `occlusion_lockstep.ancestries`) gating BOTH the
crosswalk and `--ld-file`; off the list the declared file is **invisible** (never even
`file.exists()`-tested), proven by `identical()` on the **entire matrix object**. Kill switch:
`ld_read_path.ancestries: []`.

**A SECOND WRONG-LOCUS DEFECT, caught during the replan.** The crosswalk selected M2 regions by
smallest containing span on `start_grch37`/`end_grch37` — but for a **split parent** those columns
are the PARENT's ~89 Mb bounding box copied verbatim into every subregion row. All 18 subregions of
`m2_region_00040` tied exactly, and the lexicographic tie-break returned `__sub00`, whose real
GRCh37 window has **ZERO overlap with SH2B3 and sits ~66 Mb away** — the Track A ANCHOR locus. It
survived a *"12/12 independently reproduced"* oracle because that reproduction re-implemented the
**same mechanism**. Fixed by lifting candidate windows GRCh38→GRCh37 and ranking by core overlap;
oracle re-derived and independently re-implemented: **exactly 1 of 12 moved** (`__sub00` → `__sub14`).
⚠ The mechanism was broken for **all 8 split parents** (123 of 276 AFR regions).

**THE FOUR BLOCKERS.** **(A)** `--ld-file` was a *preference*, not a mandate — `run_susie_rss.R:216`
only returned a candidate passing `overlap>=50 && coverage>=0.5`, else fell through to the 1kG panel
reporting `ld_loaded;overlap_ok`. **NOW:** declared panel is the SOLE candidate when authoritative,
and `assert_declared_ld_authoritative()` **HARD-STOPS** on unreadable / `use_identity` /
below-min-use. `stop()` over a status field **deliberately**: a written JSON + rc 0 makes Snakemake
mark the job DONE — the exact failure class of this sweep (the `resolved == opened` receipt already
existed and nobody read it); `stop()` makes the DAG the gate, `--keep-going` bounds it to one region.

gone from the `.rds`, the read is block-bounded (maxRSS **0.351×** on real banded panels), `mem_mb`

## 2026-07-04 — Seth Defect 1 landed (quick-260703-vk9: snplist-read race guard + skip-when-no-drop); AoU loop STILL RUNNING (do NOT re-fire)

> **quick-260703-vk9 (Seth Defect 1) LANDED + GREEN — NC-State-side, CODE ONLY.** The live region-1 loop error (`.ld.bin implies 102421 but the window .bim has 0 rows`) was a **RACE, not a logic bug** (Seth, offline-verified): `_retained_window_bim` read `{out_prefix}.snplist` with a bare `read_text()` and **no** retry-on-zero guard (unlike the sibling raw-window `.bim` read), racing plink's flush → an empty snplist → 0 retained ids → a false `n_var` mismatch. > **Fix (3 edits, `src/python/run_native_ld_panel.py` ONLY):** (A) `_needs_retained_subset(bin_n_var, raw_window_n_var)` — the snplist∩.bim intersection runs ONLY when `--mac` actually dropped variants (`bin != raw`); in the observed AFR regime `--mac 1` drops 0 → SKIP the intersection (and its race), use the already-race-guarded raw window `.bim`; (B) `_retained_window_bim` gains `expect_nonzero` → a bounded retry on the snplist read mirroring `_window_bim_n_var_retry_on_zero` (loud WARN on recovery; a persistently-empty snplist still returns `[]` → the caller's byte-identical `n_var` mismatch still fires — **nothing loosened**); (C) the square caller is now conditional. **TDD RED→GREEN, `tests/m3/test_defect1.py` (3 cases); full `tests/m3` 336 passed / 30 skipped** (baseline 333 + 3). > **⚠ THE AOU LOOP IS STILL RUNNING on the VM (`2d3d67`) AND MUST NOT BE RE-FIRED** — this fix is NC-State-side, NOT on the VM. The LOOP-RUNNING state in the 2026-07-03 / 2026-07-02 blocks below remains the authoritative in-flight concern (liveness = bucket `.npz` → 276; do NOT restart the kernel; STOP-on-complete at 276).

## 2026-06-30 (SUPERSEDED by 2026-07-01 above — the "transient" diagnosis was WRONG; real cause = monomorphic-NaN symmetry failure) — FIRE #3 (n1-standard-32 / 120 GB, `1b45d43`) PROVED BOTH OOM FIXES, then hit what I mis-diagnosed as a transient `.bim` short-read on region 1 → banked 0/276 (resume-safe). **The OOM fixes are exonerated; the 4th 0/276 cause was a transient, not a code bug.** The AoU UI offered no 128 GB option (only n1-standard-8..96, n2-standard-8/16), and n1-standard-64 (240 GB) busts the $500 cap — so Carter (AskUserQuestion) chose **n1-standard-32 (32 vCPU / 120 GB)**; the real RAM need is the ~40 GiB dense matrix, and 120 GB clears it to ~168k vars (region 1 = 102,421). Fire #3 (PID 432, HEAD `1b45d43`) reached region 1: plink emitted the 102,421-var `.ld.bin` (41,960,244,964 B), and the `.npz` **convert AND `content_verify_npz` both ran with NO `dmesg` OOM** — **both OOM fixes (`3eac803` + `ff9e66f`) are now proven on real data at scale**. But region 1 recorded `status=error: n_var mismatch ... the window .bim has 0 rows`.

**~6 read-only dig rounds PROVED no static defect.** Ruled out, in order: (1) stale/modified src — `md5 e99fd817…` == committed `1b45d43`; (2) chr-literal verify — the deployed `_window_bim_n_var` uses `_chrom_match_key` on both sides (line 284) and returns 102421 on the real `.bim`; (3) function regression `a5a5f9f`→`1b45d43` — byte-identical (fire #2 ran the SAME fn and PASSED this check, reaching the convert); (4) manifest float64 promotion — manifest `md5 62d1fb0…` == committed, `chr` dtype `int64`, no NaN, region1 chr = `int 1`; (5) interpreter/pandas divergence — loop `python` == repro `python3` == `/opt/conda/envs/jupyter`, pandas 2.3.2; (6) **determinism** — the EXACT driver path (`_read_manifest` → AFR `r1` → `_window_bim_n_var` → `_n_var_from_ld_bin` on the intact `.ld.bin`) **REPLAYS to `window_n_var=102421 == bin_n_var=102421` (MATCH)**. The float-chrom mechanism (`_chrom_match_key(1.0)='1.0'≠'1'`→0 rows) was *proven possible but ruled out* (runtime chr is int). Cohort `.bim` full+stable (883,834,306 B, mtime **Jun 27**, 20,767,864 lines). **Conclusion: a one-off TRANSIENT short read of `bim_path.read_text()` at the instant plink finished the 42 GB `.ld.bin` write.** Non-reproducible, no code bug. (Lesson reinforced: verify the runtime cause on real data before shipping — an early "verify does a chr-literal compare" AND a "`_read_manifest` reads float" hypothesis were both DISPROVEN on the VM before any code was touched, per `[[feedback_verify_assumption_before_shipping]]`.)

**The loop is already resume-safe per region** — an errored region has no `.npz` → it recomputes on re-fire; fire #3 shows "0/276" only because we STOPPED it (`kill 432`) at region 1 to investigate (left alone it would have banked ~275/276). Carter chose to add a **guard** so a transient self-heals IN-run.

**GUARD (quick `260630-rn4`, LANDED + GREEN):** reusable `_window_bim_n_var_retry_on_zero(...expect_nonzero...)` — when the square-path window read returns 0 but plink emitted a non-trivial `.ld.bin` (`bin_n_var>0`), re-read the `.bim` up to 3× (0.5 s sleep); a recovery logs a LOUD `WARN` and proceeds; a persistent 0 returns unchanged so the caller raises the **byte-identical** `ValueError` mismatch (`status=error`, loop continues — no semantics change). Banded path / resume / `content_verify_npz` / OOM checks untouched. **T1 tests `bba9cf8` (RED) + T2 impl `27af416` (GREEN)** (`src/python/run_native_ld_panel.py` lines 110/301/514); full `tests/m3` **309 passed / 30 skipped** (baseline 305 + 4 new); mismatch `ValueError` byte-identical (git diff shows no +/- on the string). ## 2026-06-29 (SUPERSEDED by 2026-06-30 above — the 128 GB respec plan is OBSOLETE; fire #3 ran on 120 GB and PROVED both OOM fixes) — RE-FIRED LOOP OOM-KILLED ON REGION 1 (banked 0/276 AGAIN — different bug): the `.npz` convert step blew 64 GiB on a full-matrix symmetry check; FIXED + pushed (`3eac803`); VM STOPPED by Carter; re-fire on **n2-highmem-16 (128 GB)**

**The chr-prefix fix worked — a NEW bug surfaced one step later.** The 2026-06-28 re-fire (PID 6577, `a5a5f9f`) reached region 1, and plink's `--r square bin4` compute **finished and emitted the 102,421-variant `.ld.bin`** (the chr-prefix fix is **proven** — verify agreed, the window matched). But the very next step — the Python `.ld.bin`→`.npz` convert/bank — **OOM-killed** (`anon-rss ~61 GiB` on the 64 GB VM). Banked **0/276** again, for an unrelated reason. `df` was clean (42%) — this was a **RAM** ceiling, not the disk/chr-prefix issues.

**Root cause (confirmed):** in `src/python/plink_ld_to_npz.py`, `read_square_bin()` verified symmetry with `np.allclose(m, m.T, atol=1e-4)`. At n_var=102,421 the dense float32 matrix `m` is ~39 GiB; `np.allclose(m, m.T)` materializes **several full `n_var²` float32 temporaries** (`m-m.T`, `abs(...)`, `atol+rtol*abs(b)`, the bool mask), each ~39 GiB, **on top of** `m` → peak >64 GiB → kernel OOM-kill. **`np.savez_compressed` is NOT the killer** (numpy streams the array into the zip in chunks); the diagonal check is tiny. Same dense-collect OOM class the Hail path already guarded (`_max_safe_to_numpy_n_var`/`_dense_footprint_bytes`); the native-plink converter never got that guard. Third format/memory bug in this loop (after triangle-flag + chr-prefix) → per `[[feedback_extract_reusable_utilities]]` the fix is a reusable utility + failing-first regression.

**Fix landed + (to be) PUSHED — commit `3eac803`:** added module-level `_is_symmetric_blocked(m, atol, block=1024)` — compares `block` rows at a time against the matching transposed column block, bounding the transient to ~`block*n_var*4` bytes (~420 MB at n_var≈1e5) while enforcing the **identical** invariant. `read_square_bin` line ~171 now calls it; the `ValueError` message is **byte-identical**, the diagonal check + no-copy `reshape(...).astype(copy=False)` view are unchanged. Region-1 peak after the fix ≈ 45 GiB. **TDD (failing-first):** 3 new tests (`_is_symmetric_blocked` accept/reject; agreement with `np.allclose(m, m.T)` across block-boundary edge cases incl. `block>n`; `read_square_bin` still raises on asymmetric input). Targeted `tests/m3/test_plink_ld_to_npz.py` **17 passed**; full `tests/m3` **302 passed / 30 skipped** (above the 287+ baseline; R/reticulate loader tests RAN via the `m3-r-ld` Rscript pin; 30 skips are pre-existing Hail/synthetic-MT/chain gates). Quick task `260629-2im`.

**★ BLAST-RADIUS CAUGHT A SECOND OOM HEAD (fixed @ `ff9e66f`, quick `260629-402`) — the `260629-2im` fix alone would have OOM'd the re-fire AGAIN.** A 4-investigator read-only blast-radius sweep of `3eac803` confirmed the predicate is provably equivalent (927 cases, 0 disagreements) AND the `.npz`/callers/resume are all safe — but two investigators independently found that `content_verify_npz` (the in-process per-region D-M3-10 gate, `src/python/run_native_ld_panel.py:228`, run right after the convert at `:475`) RELOADS the full `.npz` (`np.load` → ~39 GiB) and re-runs the **same unbounded** `np.allclose(ld, ld.T, atol=1e-4)` → **~4× matrix ≈ 156 GiB at n_var=102,421 > the 128 GB VM** → region 1 would OOM-kill again, one function later (0/276). Fix @ `ff9e66f`: square branch → reuse `pln._is_symmetric_blocked`; banded branch (`:234`, same class via a full `np.triu` copy — off the square path but in the same shared gate) → new co-located `pln._strict_upper_is_zero_blocked` (block-wise `np.triu(block, k=i+1)`). Reason strings byte-identical; diagonal + flag checks untouched. TDD failing-first; targeted **48 passed**; full `tests/m3` **305 passed / 30 skipped** (≥302 baseline). **Both OOM heads on the square convert→verify path are now bounded** (peak ≈ matrix + ~0.5 GiB ≈ 40 GiB for region 1). Non-blocking notes from the sweep: SIGKILL writes no panel row + `peak_ram_gib` measures the plink child not the Python convert → monitor liveness by bucket `.npz` count + `grep Killed`/137, not the panel TSV; resume skip-guard trusts only the 256 B byte-floor (pre-existing, not region-1-relevant). **Re-fire now requires `git pull` of `ff9e66f` (≥ both fixes), not just `3eac803`.**

**VM STOPPED by Carter** (idle-billing halted; stop ≠ delete — VM + reattachable PD + 379 GB local bfile preserved, resume-safe). **Carter DECIDED: re-fire on `n2-highmem-16` (128 GB)** — the lean fix makes region 1 fit on 64 GB (~45 GiB peak), but the bare dense array scales `n_var²`, so an unknown region >~115k variants could still crowd a 64 GB box deep into the ~11-day run; 128 GB gives headroom up to ~175k vars. ~$60-67 total (was ~$45-50), under the $500 cap.

## 2026-06-28 (SUPERSEDED by the 2026-06-29 OOM-fix block above — the loop it describes reached region 1 then OOM-killed on the `.npz` convert; the chr-prefix fix it landed is PROVEN GOOD) ~05:50Z — LOOP RE-FIRED on the FIXED code: the chr-prefix verify bug that left the first fire banking 0/276 is fixed + pushed (a5a5f9f); the loop is live again on the same VM, STOP-on-complete ordered (stop, NOT delete)

**The first fire banked 0/276 — fixed, re-fired clean.** The 2026-06-27 loop (PID 4237) ran ~17h and banked **nothing**: region 1 failed verify `n_var mismatch: .ld.bin implies 102421 but the window .bim has 0 rows`. **Root cause (confirmed on real AoU data):** the AoU GRCh38 cohort `.bim` uses **`chr`-prefixed** contigs (`chr1`) while `config/ld_regions.tsv` `chr` is **bare numeric** (`1`). plink1.9 normalizes the prefix (so `--chr 1` correctly built the 102,421-var `.ld.bin`), but `_window_bim_n_var` did a **literal** `str(parts[0]) == chrom` compare that never strips `chr` → 0 in-window rows → mismatch on every region. The fake-plink test stub `_n_var_in_window` used the **identical** literal compare → CI was blind.

**Fix landed + PUSHED (origin == local == `a5a5f9f`):** `_chrom_match_key()` (strip leading `chr`/`CHR`, mirroring plink1.9) applied to **both** sides of the compare in `src/python/run_native_ld_panel.py`; kept-line content stays **verbatim** (downstream `ld_npz_to_rds.R` strips the prefix before the bare-`chr:pos` GWAS join, so no second mis-keying bug). Failing-first parametrized regression over all 4 prefix combos; `tests/m3` **28 + 14 passed**. Commits `baeb925` (fix+test) / `a5a5f9f` (docs). Quick task `260628-244`. Also repaired a pre-existing **git index dangling-blob corruption** (`.planning/WAVE-1-CLOSEOUT-CHECKLIST.md`, object missing from the store) via `git hash-object -w` to unblock the docs commit.

**RE-FIRED clean (PID 6577, 05:44:59 UTC) on VM `AoU_Jupyter_ComputeEngine_20260626b`** (n2-standard-16, 1TB, $0.83/hr, Hail-free), `nohup` + `timeout 312h`, server-side. AoU agent `git pull`ed to `a5a5f9f`, **pre-flight confirmed region-1 chr1 window = 102,421** (== plink log) → verify will now agree → banking will succeed. Baseline: `npz_count=0`, `df` 391G/554G (42%).

**Liveness = climbing bucket `.npz` count** `gsutil ls gs://rw-migration-aou-rw-476cdac2/ld/afr_native_panel/*.npz | wc -l` → **276** (NOT the kernel/log). **Do NOT restart the kernel** — nohup'd + resume-safe. **The fix's proof point = region 1 (~06:42 UTC) + regions 1–3:** `npz` 0→1→2→3 **and** `df` returns to ~42% after each bank (reclaim deletes the `.ld.bin`; the bank→reclaim cycle proves both the verify fix and reclaim). Debug session resolved → `.planning/debug/resolved/m3-W2-a2-dense-narrow-driver-oom-routing.md` + knowledge-base append. 0.40 fraction is conservative-correct (the float32 astype copy co-resides → true transient peak `n²×12`).

## 2026-06-18 — dev-10 LD fire KILLED: intractable on the dev cluster; Wave-2 RE-SCOPE needed (the finding that triggered the re-scope above)

**The dev-10 LD fire is DONE — killed, 0 regions completed. It proved a CAPACITY wall, not a correctness bug.** Cluster STOPPED, billing halted, orphan scratch deleted.

### What happened

- dev-10 fired on `20260617` (16× n2-standard-4 = 64 vCPU / 16 GB workers). **`region_00006`** (the *cheapest* A.3 region: n_var=122,678, AFR 73,122 samples) crawled its 900-block Stage-4 at **~0.9–2.1 blocks/min** (vs **~66** for the synthetic repro on the same cluster), and the **64 GB master CRASHED** during its **~65 GiB dense-scratch** phase. `region_00040` (xlarge, ×2) would be **~weeks/cell**. Killed; **0 regions completed**, no run log.
- **Root cause:** the repro used `--n-samples 2000`; the real cohorts are **73,122 / 220,098** samples, and the correlation matmul's inner dim **is** the sample count → **~36× (AFR) / ~110× (EUR)** more compute per block + 16 GB workers spilling + master can't sustain the dense scratch. The repro validated the **fix's correctness** (no driver-collect *hang*) but its 928 s wall-time **never represented real-cohort COST** — and we sized the fire off it.

### Settled (do NOT relitigate)

- **A.3 fix is CORRECT** (materialize-then-band; blocks advanced, no hang) — confirmed on the repro and region_00006. The problem is **capacity**, not the fix.
- **Ordering A vs B is settled** (keep A) — banded==dense under the radius scheme; **splitting**, not re-ordering, shrinks the dense intermediate.
- **Cohorts intact**, read-gate + bucket wiring proven (AFR 73,122 / EUR 220,098 read live).

### NEXT (RESUME HERE) — Wave-2 re-scope, NOT a re-fire

**2026-06-18 (resume) — DECISION: plan the FULL re-scope before any re-fire.** Carter confirmed the bigger-cluster path, and we agreed that a resize alone is insufficient because dev-10 itself contains `region_00040` (xlarge) — so the xlarge-region split (change #2) must land before the re-fire. Proceeding to `/gsd-plan-phase`.

Run **`/gsd-plan-phase`** for the re-scope per **`.planning/phases/m3-aou-afr-ld-panel-build/WAVE-2-RESCOPE-real-cohort-compute.md`** — three coupled changes:

1. **Cluster sized for real cohorts** — n2-highmem-16 workers (RAM stops the spill), 256–384 vCPU (push to quota), highmem master (the dense scratch crashed a 64 GB master).
2. **Split the ~16 xlarge regions** into ≤~75–100k-var sub-regions (drops cross-sub-region LD beyond the 50 Mb radius — already the accepted banded treatment; shrinks compute + scratch + driver load at once; the **load-bearing** change — resize alone does NOT make xlarge tractable). Touches `build_ld_region_manifest.py` / `compute_region_ld` / `ld_npz_to_rds.R`.
3. **Redo the ~1,117 cluster-h cost model** from real per-region rates (EUR ~3× AFR/block).

Teardown: orphan `…/ld/AFR_aou/bm/m2_region_00006.corr_scratch.bm` (~65 GiB) DELETED; cluster `20260617` STOPPED (synced repo + hail preserved if boot disk persists, else re-sync via GitHub ZIP). - **Liveness = data layer:** Spark app `application_1781785150907_0001` advancing + `gsutil ls -r …/ld/AFR_aou/ …/ld/EUR_aou/` for landed outputs + `gsutil du -s …/<region>.bm/parts/` growing for the in-flight region.

- **Fail-fast monitoring gate (agreed protocol):** the loop runs cheap regions first (`00006` → `00027`) before the expensive `00040`. Verify `00006` (and `00027`) at the data layer as they land — if a cheap region comes back **bad/empty**, **Spark-UI job-kill before `00040`** burns ~1.4 TiB / hours; if they're good, `00040` is green to proceed. Don't wait until the end to evaluate.
- **All 10 land + verify → AOU-4 validation memo** (`.planning/notebooks/AOU-4_validation.ipynb`); `region_00040` numbers → GATE-3 cost memo (likely "accept the cost", no region-splitting).

## 2026-06-18 — A.3 ordering experiment DONE on cluster; fix VALIDATED; KEEP ordering A; B RETIRED; CR-01 reframed (SUPERSEDED by the dev-10 fire block above)

**The A.3 BlockMatrix-write hang is FIXED + CLUSTER-VALIDATED. The ordering experiment ran and the decision is made: KEEP ordering A, retire ordering B.** Quick `260618-3at`.

### What ran

- Cluster `20260617` (the 2-worker repro cluster) was **resized 2 → 16 workers** (n2-standard-4; the machine type is locked on resize, so we scaled count). `MASTER=yarn`, 64 concurrent tasks. The resize succeeding **diagnosed the original 24-worker prod-cluster failure as quota/size-bound, NOT project-level** (perimeter/credit).
- `scripts/a3_blockmatrix_lowering_repro.py --skip-old --n-var 130000 --n-samples 2000 --radius-bp 1000000 --budget-sec 3600`:
  - **Ordering A COMPLETED — 928.0 s**, valid `A/repro_A.bm`. The 21 s gap between the dense checkpoint and the final write = the mechanism working (final write reads concrete blocks). **First cluster validation of the deployed A.3 fix** (dev-10 paused before ever confirming it).
  - **Ordering B COMPLETED — 863.5 s**, valid `B/repro_B.bm`. B's `sparsify→checkpoint` (same IR shape as the OLD hung write) **did NOT reproduce the hang** — IR-shape caveat cleared.
  - OLD skipped (hang already proven by dev-10 + the prior 2-worker 0.21 MiB/s driver-bound grind).
- **DECISIVE (`--report-scratch-size`, full manifest):** `banded(B) == dense(A)` for **ALL 23 A.3 regions** — the manifest's `radius = span+500kb` (cap 50 Mb) is always ≥ span/2, so the band covers ~the whole region everywhere → **B saves scratch NOWHERE**.

### Decision

- **KEEP ordering A** (deployed + now cluster-validated). **Ordering B RETIRED** — byte-identical numerics, zero scratch benefit, nonzero change-risk. The `DRAFT-orderingB-band-before-checkpoint.md` patch is parked (revisit only if a future manifest adopts radius ≪ span).
- **CR-01 is ORDERING-INDEPENDENT:** region_00145 (~710k var) needs ~1.8 TiB of transient GCS scratch under A *or* B. The debug session `m3-W2-a3-blockmatrix-write-ir-lowering-hang` is **RESOLVED** (the hang bug is fixed + cluster-confirmed).
- Cluster `20260617` being **Stopped** (not deleted — preserves the verified script + `hail==0.2.135` install).

### NEXT (RESUME HERE)

- **GATE-2 dev-10 can resume on ordering A** (the fix is validated; ~60 GB scratch for one dev region is tolerable). Re-fire the previously-hanging A.3 cell (`m2_region_00006`), verify the `.bm` at the **data layer** (`_assert_blockmatrix_written` shape + `gsutil du` of `.bm/parts`, NOT `_SUCCESS`) → resume the rest of dev-10 → AOU-4 validation memo.
- **GATE-3 open item (separate scoping task, ordering-independent):** decide whether the xlarge A.3 regions' ~1.8 TiB dense materialize is acceptable on **transient GCS scratch + compute cost**, or whether the handful of >50 Mb regions need **splitting / a streaming band-by-band write**. Scope = estimate region_00145's dense-materialize wall-time + cost on the production cluster, then choose "accept cost" vs "split". This is the real GATE-3 blocker now — NOT checkpoint ordering.

GATE-1.5 (cohorts) ✅ · GATE-0 (egress) ✅ · GATE-1 (cost) ✅ · GATE-2 (dev-10) 🟡 unblocked, ready to resume on ordering A · GATE-3 (322-cell) 🔴 BLOCKED on the ordering-independent xlarge dense-materialize cost question.

The adversarial pass (verified vs Hail source) established: (a) the lowering **warning is NOT eliminated** — per `CanLowerEfficiently.scala` EVERY BlockMatrix write (incl. the checkpoint) runs interpreted; the fix is cheap because the final write reads **concrete materialized blocks**, not an un-materialized matmul; (b) **CR-01 = a GATE-3 blocker**: the current ordering (A) checkpoints the FULL DENSE n×n correlation → ~2 TB scratch on the largest region (m2_region_00145, ~710k var); (c) **Pan-UKBB uses ordering B (band-then-checkpoint, ~GB scratch) at biobank scale → B is the leading production default**. Default code stays A pending the cluster experiment.

- **Gate B blocker #2 — driver-collect stall** in `load_qc_cohort` (the real query-shape bug), root-caused + FIXED via `/gsd-debug` (HIGH confidence). The pre-write `mt.repartition(2048)` (`shuffle=True`) after `split_multi_hts` built a Spark RangePartitioner by **sampling row keys across all partitions** → a driver-side `SpillingCollectIterator` (jstack: `TableValue.mapRows`) funneled ~2,077 partitions to the master, spilling to `/tmp` (~38 min, ~1.4s driver CPU, 0 executor threads). Doubly redundant (naive_coalesce already set count=2048; the checkpoint write+reread already yields a clean partitioner). **Fix** (Hail-team "repartition after write, not before"): drop the pre-write repartition; rebalance on the post_split checkpoint **read-back** via `hl.read_matrix_table(ckpt, _n_partitions=_post_split_read_partitions(...))` — on-disk partition index, no key sampling, no driver gather. New pure helper `_post_split_read_partitions` + `_COHORT_TARGET_PARTITIONS=2048`. Commits **`e23c081`** (code+tests) + **`0f92428`** (debug doc `.planning/debug/m3-gateb-load-qc-cohort-driver-collect.md`). 3 RED-first regression guards; tests/m3 **112 passed / 27 skipped**; Track-4 guard `_assert_checkpoint_nonempty` byte-identical (verified via diff scope). **This bug would also have crippled the full genome-wide fire.**
- **INTERVAL defaults to whole `chr22`** — the 2,077 surviving partitions ≈ whole chr22, so the stall was observed at the expensive scale. For the cheap re-fire, set `INTERVAL='chr22:16000000-18000000'` explicitly.
- **Driver-collect fix LIVE-CONFIRMED (re-fire #1).** On the n2-standard-16 cluster, state=FRESH wrote the post_split intermediate — **120 real row partitions**, **no stall**, **0 spills**, ~3 min (vs the 38-min wedge), clean driver jstack, no empty-MT catastrophe. The fix works in production.
- **Gate B blocker #3 — colon-in-path (latent, exposed by the read-back) — FIXED + pushed + NCSU-verified.** Re-fire #1 errored after post_split on `java.net.URISyntaxException: Relative path in absolute URI: mt_afr_post_split_chr22:16000000-18000000.mt` — the nano INTERVAL's colon leaked into `_intermediate_checkpoint_uri`; GCS tolerated it on write but the new read-back routed it through Hadoop's `Path` parser. **Fix:** reusable `_sanitize_interval_suffix()` (`:`/`-`→`_`, matching the AOU-1 notebook + final-output convention) applied in `_intermediate_checkpoint_uri` (the lone affected builder; scope-scanned). Intermediate is now `mt_afr_post_split_chr22_16000000_18000000.mt`. RED reproduced the exception NCSU-side; GREEN after; **tests/m3 116 passed / 27 skipped** (+4); driver-collect fix + Track-4 guard byte-identical/intact; functionally proven on the exact failing input. ### Pre-2026-05-06 narrative (preserved for context)

**Pivot adopted 2026-04-22.** Project reframed from candidate-locus design (50 hand-curated regions, circular by construction per Amendment §2.3) to **genome-wide joint-signal discovery across 9 traits × 2 ancestries** (Amendment §§2, 4). Milestone sequence M0–M6 replaces the prior T1/T2/T3 tier structure.

**Stage 2 fire numerics (2026-04-22 AM, `bin/fire_phase2_stage2_refit.sh`):**

- 51/96 non-empty real-LD credible sets vs 48/95 under the matched-coverage k2d full-coverage identity-LD comparator (2026-04-25 re-fire) = 1.06× yield contrast (SUPERSEDED 2026-04-25 narrow-validation 12/96 / 4.25× baseline preserved in `.planning/amendments/TRACK-A-FROZEN-NUMBERS.md`).
- 0 Tier A signals at genome-wide-significance thresholds.
- Flagship SH2B3 × asthma EUR coloc: identity-LD PP.H4 = 1.0 → real-LD n_cs_a = 0 (Benner 2017 identity-LD inflation, now demonstrated on a canonical-literature signal).
- 861 hard failures in the pairwise trait-pair sweep (to be quantified in Track A frozen-numbers pass).

**Two-track split:**

- **Track A** — short-form real-LD audit paper of the candidate-locus design. Venue ladder: *Genome Medicine* → *AJHG* short report → *Bioinformatics* Applications Note. First-pass draft landed 2026-04-23 (commit `bde60e2`) at `docs/manuscript/id-vs-ref-LD.md`. Strategy in `.planning/amendments/ID-VS-REF-LD-STRATEGY.md`.
- **Track B** — genome-wide 9-trait × 2-ancestry joint-signal discovery with MTAG + CPASSOC + HyPrColoc + PolyFun + All-of-Us controlled-tier AFR WGS LD panel (~100k AFR). Target: *Nature Genetics*. Planning lives under `.planning/amendments/PROJECT-AMENDMENT-2026-04-22-genome-wide-reframe.md` §§3, 5, 6, 7.

**Four parallel routes** (per `/home/ckclinto/.claude/plans/snappy-humming-pine.md`):

- **Route A** — Track A manuscript push: freeze Tier counts → Section 4.1–4.20 edits → figures → bioRxiv.
- **Route B** — M0 closeout: PROJECT.md / ROADMAP.md / REQUIREMENTS.md / DECISIONS.md rewrites per Amendment §12, then OSF amendment posted (hard gate on M2 per Amendment §9.1).
- **Route C** — Track B M1 sumstats upgrade: `bin/download_sumstats_v2.sh` driver already running on URL-fetchable sources (Aragam 2022, CKDGen 2019, GLGC 2021 landed); manual-fetch queue in `.planning/amendments/SUMSTATS-MANUAL-FETCH.md` awaits Carter portal actions.
- **Route D** — this hygiene pass (Step 0; fills the gap between 2026-04-21 stale state and the pivot-era repo).

| 260501-v9q | **CR-001 regression pytest + tests/m3 scaffold parity — locks the post-CR-001 resolver behavior (independent `region_id` and `region_safe` placeholders) behind a fail-fast regression-bite test.** Closes the regression-prevention loop opened by the m3-aou-afr-ld-panel-build code-review fix pass (commit `6d2e753`, CR-001 finding). **Pre-CR-001 root cause:** `resolve_ld_path()` substituted both `{region_id}` and `{region_safe}` placeholders with the same `region_id` value; the AoU chain head template uses `{region_id}` (e.g., `m2_region_00067.rds`) while 1kg/HGDP/UKBB tails use `{region_safe}` (e.g., `FTO_16q12.rds`), so the bug silently caused the 1kg fallback to never resolve when only `region_safe`-named files existed on disk. **What this task adds:** 3 regression tests appended to `tests/m3/test_ld_panel_resolver.py` (8 → 11 tests; existing 8 byte-identical except one new `import re`): (1) `test_resolver_distinct_region_id_and_region_safe_aou_head` — positive AoU-head contract with DISTINCT `region_id="m2_region_00067"` + `region_safe="FTO_16q12"`, asserts AoU-bucket `.rds` resolves; (2) `test_resolver_distinct_region_id_and_region_safe_1kg_fallback` — **the decisive regression-bite test**: AoU bucket EMPTY + 1kg has only `FTO_16q12.rds`; asserts resolver returns the 1kg path. Confirmed via temporary pre-fix simulation in `src/python/ld_panel.py` line 85 (single-arg substitution restored): test B FAILS with `FileNotFoundError: No LD panel found for m2_region_00067 AFR`; fix restored, all 11 pass. (3) `test_finemap_smk_calls_resolver_with_both_kwargs` — static-grep guard against future call-site regressions: regex-asserts that `src/snakemake/rules/finemap.smk` calls `resolve_ld_path` with both `region_id=` and `region_safe=` kwargs. Without this static check, a refactor that drops one kwarg would re-introduce the silent fallback bug because the resolver's back-compat default (`region_safe=None` → `region_safe=region_id`) would mask it. **Nuance recorded in SUMMARY:** Test A (AoU-head) does NOT bite pre-fix code in isolation because the AoU template uses ONLY `{region_id}` — substituting both placeholders with `region_id` still resolves the AoU file correctly. Test B is the only regression-bite test; Test A is retained as a positive AoU-contract guard, Test C as static call-site coverage. **Hermetic:** `tmp_path` + `Path.touch()`; no Hail, no AoU, no network; reuses existing `_build_config` and `_touch` helpers (no duplication). **Scaffold parity:** added empty `tests/m3/__init__.py` aligning the m3 test directory with the precedent set by `tests/m1/__init__.py` (m2 still lacks one — out of scope for this task). **1 atomic commit, scoped via explicit `git add <path>`:** `66d6b8f` `test(quick-260501-v9q): add CR-001 regression tests for ld_panel resolver`. **Verification:** `pytest tests/m3/test_ld_panel_resolver.py -v` → **11 passed in 0.50s** (Python 3.11.15 / `/rs1/researchers/c/ckclinto/conda_envs/smoke_dev/bin/python`). **Project rules honored:** explicit-path staging only (no `git add .` / `-A`); no conda activation guidance to user; HPC PATH preface used in verification. Pre-existing dirty files (`.claude/settings.json`, `.planning/config.json`, parallel-workstream `results_lsweep_*` dirs from ta-sh2b3 SuSiE max-iter sweep, `wave4_dispatch_tracker_v4_addendum_supervisor_orphan.json`, `260429-utt/bjobs.tsv`) NOT staged. **Out-of-scope (not addressed by this task):** other CR-001 sibling concerns (CR-002 Hail row-order verification, CR-003/CR-004 follow-ups), the 3 skipped-from-fix-pass items (WR-005/WR-006/WR-009 — see REVIEW-FIX.md for rationales), tests/m2 scaffold parity (m2 also lacks `__init__.py` but is unrelated to CR-001). | 260512-ldj | **NCSU AOU-1 reference template synced to AoU-working init pattern — audit-driven re-analysis closing the stale-template producer/consumer drift surfaced 2026-05-12.** Follow-on to quick 260512-jd9 (helper extraction + checkpoint-URI patch). The NCSU canonical reference notebook `.planning/notebooks/AOU-1_template.ipynb` Cell 1 carried the old `init_hail()` invocation that's empirically broken on AoU's Dataproc + YARN cluster: `hl.init(spark_conf={"spark.executor.cores": "1"})` is silently overridden by the cluster's spark-defaults.conf + YARN `yarn.scheduler.minimum-allocation-vcores` floor, yielding `spark.executor.cores=2` in the live SparkConf despite the dict-supplied conf — defeating the cores=1 belt-and-suspenders pair of DEC-2026-05-04-01's `mt.naive_coalesce(2048)` v8 partition-explosion OOM remediation (commit `8cc6f64`). Carter confirmed empirically 2026-05-12 during the Cell 2 init fire that the working lever is `PYSPARK_SUBMIT_ARGS="--conf spark.executor.cores=1 pyspark-shell"` set BEFORE any pyspark/hail import — this applies at the spark-submit boundary, which beats spark-defaults.conf in Spark's config-precedence order. Canonical pattern baked into auto-memory `[[aou-dataproc-pyspark-submit-args]]` for cross-session persistence. **Edit strategy:** Python helper script (NOT hand-edit JSON) — load `.ipynb` via `json.load`, pre-state assert (8 cells, cell[1] contains `init_hail()`), split cell[1] into two new cells (Cell 1a PYSPARK_SUBMIT_ARGS injection + Cell 1b direct hl.init with spark_conf dict + verification asserts on cores=1 and `_qc_checkpoint_uri` import from commit `36e8062`), append sync-note paragraph to cell[0] markdown title, post-state assert (9 cells), `json.dump(indent=1)` to preserve nbformat conventions. **Cell-count delta:** 8 → 9 (cell[1] split into cell[1a]+cell[1b], net +1; cells 2-7 byte-identical; cell[0] markdown appended). **Verification (all 10 PASS):** json.load asserts 9 cells / `PYSPARK_SUBMIT_ARGS` grep = 7 / `spark.executor.cores` grep = 5 / `_qc_checkpoint_uri` grep = 6 / `init_hail()` grep = 0 (old wrapper call removed) / `feedback_aou_dataproc_pyspark_submit_args` grep = 2 (markdown sync-note + Cell 1b docstring cross-ref) / `hl.init(` grep = 3 / `load_qc_cohort` grep = 6 / `len(overlap) == 0` grep = 1 / JSON validity round-trip exit 0. One in-spec anomaly: `naive_coalesce(2048)` grep = 2 (cited in Cell 1a + Cell 1b comments as pairing cross-references per canonical-pattern documentation; reviewer-friendly + reviewer-defensible). **1 atomic commit `5389a88`** with two files (.ipynb + STATE.md), pushed to `origin/main` (`d7a221a..5389a88 main -> main`). Subject token `(m3-W1-template-pyspark-submit-args-sync)`; body uses "audit-driven re-analysis" / "stale-template sync" framing per [[feedback_original_research_framing]]; zero forbidden tokens (cleanup/revision/salvage). **Why this matters (downstream consumers of the NCSU template):** future re-fires of M3 Wave 1 (e.g., AoU CDR v9 trigger; AoU workspace re-creation; reviewer replication audit; second author onboarding to the repo) will consume `.planning/notebooks/AOU-1_template.ipynb` as the canonical recipe. Pre-sync, anyone who pasted Cell 1 verbatim into a fresh AoU Workbench notebook would hit the executor RegionPool OOM during the Cell 3 fire. Post-sync, the paste-ready template self-verifies the cores=1 lever before any compute spend. **Carter next-action:** None — AoU-side live notebook already running with the working pattern this session; this sync is reproducibility / future re-fires / reviewer audit trail for the canonical reference template. **Cross-refs:** predecessor `260512-jd9` (`36e8062` `_qc_checkpoint_uri` helper extraction + 4 regression tests), `8cc6f64` (m3-W2 `naive_coalesce(2048)` RegionPool OOM remediation), DEC-2026-05-04-01 (originating decision binding the patch family), [[aou-dataproc-pyspark-submit-args]] (canonical pattern memory baked this session). | 260528-jvd | **m3-W1 Track 4 defensive-code patches — contents-validated MT write protocol lands as 7 atomic commits with TDD discipline; closes the silent-empty-MT failure class that produced the 2026-05-21 ~$2,100 catastrophe.** Patches the resume-gate hole + write-side validation gap exposed by the m3-W1 empty-MT catastrophe: Hail's `mt.checkpoint()` writes `_SUCCESS` on driver-side tasks-reported-complete accounting WITHOUT validating output contents — under `spark.executor.cores=1/mem=5g` (necessary for v8 partition-explosion OOM remediation), executor tasks can silently truncate after writing 35-byte Parquet column-metadata footer stubs, leaving an MT directory with `_SUCCESS` + rows-stubs + absent `entries/entries/parts/` + `count_rows()=0 + count_cols()=0` on read-back. The refactored `load_qc_cohort` at HEAD did NOT defend against this — `_has_checkpoint()` only checks `_SUCCESS`, so a stub MT triggered `RESUME_FROM_POST_SAMPLE_QC` and Phase 3 fired `variant_qc` on an empty MT, producing another empty `_SUCCESS`-marked MT. **7 atomic commits with TDD RED→GREEN sequence:** (1) `59e914b` test(m3-W1, RED) — 3 regression tests for `_validate_checkpoint_populated` (`rejects_stub_entries` / `rejects_empty_entries_dir` / `has_checkpoint_vs_validate_diverge_on_stub_mt`); shared `_make_stub_mt` helper builds the exact catastrophe pattern (`_SUCCESS` + `metadata.json.gz` + 35-byte Parquet-footer rows-stub + optionally-empty `entries/entries/parts/`); RED state confirmed via ImportError on the not-yet-existent helper. (2) `86c8a56` feat(m3-W1, GREEN) — `_validate_checkpoint_populated(uri, *, min_entries_bytes=MIN_ENTRIES_FILE_BYTES=1024)` helper in `src/python/aou_ld_panel.py`; validates `_SUCCESS` + `entries/entries/parts/` exists + at least one entries file > 1 KB threshold (cleanly discriminates footer stubs from real partition contents); `file://` scheme uses pathlib, `gs://` defers to `hl.hadoop_ls`; defensive try/except returns False on any FS error; also amends `_has_checkpoint` docstring with WARNING clause directing new callers to the validated helper. (3) `4c75ca2` fix(m3-W1) — swap `_has_checkpoint(ckpt_post_sqc)` and `_has_checkpoint(ckpt_post_split)` calls in `load_qc_cohort`'s auto-resume state machine for `_validate_checkpoint_populated()`; adds explicit catastrophe-pattern elif branches that match "`_has_checkpoint` True AND `_validate_checkpoint_populated` False" (the W1 signature) and force `auto_fresh = True` with a loud WARN print so the stub-MT pathology surfaces in the cell's stdout. (4) `88ab0c5` feat(m3-W1) — `_assert_checkpoint_nonempty(mt, uri, *, phase)` helper + invokes after each of 3 `mt.checkpoint()` sites (intermediate 1 / intermediate 2 / final); raises `RuntimeError` on `count_rows()==0 OR count_cols()==0` with pointer to catastrophe debug doc; ~10-30 sec per checkpoint × 3 = ~1-2 min total overhead per cohort (trivial against the 60+ h monolithic-run baseline; per `[[feedback_rigor_over_speed]]`). (5) `9b4317f` feat(m3-W1) — inserts 3 new code cells in `.planning/notebooks/AOU-1_template.ipynb`: Cell 3.5 (validates `mt_afr_qc.mt`), Cell 4.5 (validates `mt_afr_pca_selfid_qc.mt`), Cell 5.5 (validates `mt_eur_qc.mt`); each fires `gsutil du -s` against the corresponding `entries/entries/parts/` subpath and asserts size > 1 GB floor; halts the notebook BEFORE downstream cells define cohorts on top of an empty MT — the exact misordering that compounded the W1 catastrophe over 65h. (6) `4b321c2` docs(m3-W1) — `.planning/WAVE-1-CLOSEOUT-CHECKLIST.md` STEP 3 upgraded from `_SUCCESS`+`metadata.json.gz` listing to contents-validating verification with explicit "CRITICAL — m3-W1 catastrophe lesson" callout at top, `entries/: OK (X.YZ GB)` pass criterion, HONEST_FINDING disposition path on catastrophe-pattern fail, and a belt-and-suspenders Hail-side read-probe from a fresh Python subprocess (NOT the original kernel — JVM-cached IR masked the W1 catastrophe). (7) `bfe5f0e` decision(m3-W1) — `D-M3-10: MT write verification protocol — contents-validated, not _SUCCESS-only` decision block in `.planning/phases/m3-aou-afr-ld-panel-build/m3-CONTEXT.md` `<decisions>` + assumption #10 echo in `<assumptions>` per the D-M3-03/D-M3-09 echo pattern; locks the protocol as a phase-level requirement spanning Waves 1+2+4+5 (no new code-side `_has_checkpoint(ckpt_*)` callers may be introduced for resume-gate semantics). **Test sweep:** 38 PASSED + 11 SKIPPED + 0 FAILED on `tests/m3/test_aou_ld_panel_local.py` via `/rs1/researchers/c/ckclinto/conda_envs/smoke_dev/bin/pytest` at every commit boundary (no regression at any patch); 11 SKIPs are the existing live-Hail tests (`@_require_hail()`) that gracefully skip on smoke_dev per [[project_python_311_pin]] and will run on the next AoU env with Hail installed. **Verification gates (7/7 PASS):** `_validate_checkpoint_populated` defined in `aou_ld_panel.py` / both resume-gate sites use validated helper / 4 `_assert_checkpoint_nonempty` occurrences (1 def + 3 callers) / 3 new regression test names present / 3 new AOU-1 cells (3.5/4.5/5.5) present per nbformat round-trip / 8 `entries/entries/parts` + catastrophe references in closeout checklist / D-M3-10 grep returns 2 (decision header + assumption echo). **Behavioral net change (resume-gate truth table):** populated MT + valid sidecar → RESUME (preserved); `_SUCCESS`-absent MT → FRESH (preserved); orphan MT (sidecar absent) → WARN + auto_fresh (preserved); sidecar mismatch → RuntimeError (preserved); ****stub MT (`_SUCCESS` + empty entries) → WARN + auto_fresh (W1 catastrophe DEFENDED — pre-patch this triggered RESUME into empty MT)****. Cost: $0 (all NCSU-side; no AoU compute). Carter-state: m3-W1 Track 4 lane CLEAR; Tracks 1 (AoU credit recovery; Abby Doyle engaged, awaiting engineering response) + 2 (1000G AFR safety-net pivot decision; deferred pending Abby's team response) + 3 (NCSU v7→v8 forensic investigation; COMPLETE) continue in parallel; patches apply REGARDLESS of which LD substrate Wave 2 ends up using. **Cross-refs:** root-cause analysis at `.planning/debug/m3-W1-empty-mt-catastrophe.md` (40 KB); patch source `.planning/quick/260521-w1-catastrophe-handoff/HANDOFF.md` §Track 4; precedent refactor `.planning/quick/260518-qcr-load-qc-cohort-resilience-refactor/260518-qcr-SUMMARY.md` (introduced the unsafe `_has_checkpoint()` resume gate that this task fixes); memories `[[feedback_aou_success_marker_not_evidence_of_data]]` + `[[feedback_hail_checkpoint_contract_violation]]` + amended `[[feedback_aou_spark_ui_stack_trace_verification]]` + `[[feedback_failed_to_honest_finding]]` (HONEST_FINDING disposition rule) + `[[feedback_rigor_over_speed]]` (cost/benefit framing). | 260628-244 | **Fix the chr-prefix mismatch that left the live 276-region AFR native-plink LD fire banking 0/276 after 17h.** The AoU agent's mid-fire diagnosis: region 1 failed `n_var mismatch: .ld.bin implies 102421 but the window .bim has 0 rows`; bank is gated on verify success, so no `.npz` was written/uploaded and the raw `.ld.bin` was never reclaimed → scratch climbed to 82% (kill was ~174 GiB from disk-full). **Root cause (confirmed on real AoU data):** the GRCh38 cohort `.bim` uses `chr`-prefixed contigs (`chr1`; region-1 chr1 window = 102,421 vars, col0 literally `chr1`) while `config/ld_regions.tsv` `chr` is bare numeric (`1`). plink1.9 normalizes the prefix so `--chr 1` correctly produced the `.ld.bin`, but `_window_bim_n_var` did a literal `str(parts[0]) == chrom` compare that never strips `chr` → 0 in-window rows → mismatch on every region. The fake-plink test stub `_n_var_in_window` used the IDENTICAL literal compare against a bare `.bim`, so CI was blind. **Fix** (`src/python/run_native_ld_panel.py`): added `_chrom_match_key()` (strip leading `chr`/`CHR`, mirroring plink1.9) applied to BOTH sides of the chrom compare; kept-line content stays verbatim (downstream `ld_npz_to_rds.R` strips the prefix before the GWAS join); temp window-`.bim` filename normalized to the bare key. **Failing-first regression** (`tests/m3/test_run_native_ld_panel.py`): `test_window_bim_n_var_chr_prefix_agnostic` parametrized over all four prefix combos — `chr12`/`12` + `12`/`chr12` returned 0 pre-fix, all four pass post-fix. `pytest tests/m3/test_run_native_ld_panel.py` 28 passed (24 prior + 4 new); `test_plink_ld_to_npz.py` 14 passed (downstream unaffected). $0 NCSU-side. **Next (operational):** push to origin `m3-W2-aou-deltas` (verify tip == local HEAD); AoU agent `git pull` → real-data pre-flight (region-1 window ≈ 102,421) → re-fire from region 1 (0 banked → clean restart). | 260629-2im | **Fix the OOM that left the re-fired 276-region AFR native-plink LD loop banking 0/276 a SECOND time — this time on the `.npz` convert step, not chr-prefix.** The 2026-06-28 re-fire (PID 6577, `a5a5f9f`) reached region 1; plink `--r square bin4` finished and emitted the 102,421-var `.ld.bin` (proving the chr-prefix fix), but the Python `.ld.bin`→`.npz` convert OOM-killed (`anon-rss ~61 GiB` on a 64 GB VM). **Root cause:** `read_square_bin()` in `src/python/plink_ld_to_npz.py` verified symmetry with `np.allclose(m, m.T, atol=1e-4)`; at n_var=102,421 the dense float32 `m` is ~39 GiB and `np.allclose(m, m.T)` materializes several full `n_var²` float32 temporaries (`m-m.T`, `abs(...)`, `atol+rtol*abs(b)`, the bool mask) on top of `m` → >64 GiB peak → kernel OOM. `np.savez_compressed` is NOT the killer (chunked write); the diagonal check is tiny. **Fix:** reusable `_is_symmetric_blocked(m, atol, block=1024)` compares block rows against the matching transposed col block, bounding the transient to ~`block*n_var*4` (~420 MB) while enforcing the identical invariant; predicate swapped at line ~171, `ValueError` byte-identical, diagonal check + no-copy reshape view unchanged (region-1 peak ≈ 45 GiB). **Failing-first regression** (`tests/m3/test_plink_ld_to_npz.py`): 3 new tests (accept/reject; agreement with `np.allclose(m, m.T)` incl. `block>n`; `read_square_bin` still raises on asymmetric input). Carter STOPPED the VM (idle-billing halted; stop ≠ delete) and DECIDED to re-fire on **n2-highmem-16 (128 GB)** for headroom on any region >~115k vars. **Next (operational):** push to origin `m3-W2-aou-deltas` (verify tip == local HEAD); AoU agent `git pull 3eac803` → UI respec to highmem → pre-flight region-1 window ≈ 102,421 → re-fire from region 1 (0 banked → clean restart) → proof point `.npz` 0→1 + `df` flat. | 260715-sqe | **Resolved the m3-07b panel-column test-vs-test contradiction — the ONE open decision the 2026-07-15 close-session escalated as a Carter call.** Two tests contradicted and no implementation satisfied both: (a) `test_run_native_ld_panel.py::test_panel_columns_include_n_dropped_occluded` (the **m3-07a RED**) requires `n_dropped_occluded` in `drv._PANEL_COLUMNS`; (b) `test_run_native_ld_panel.py::test_panel_tsv_append_resume_safe` (**PRE-EXISTING**) pinned `list(df.columns)` to exactly 8 columns without it. `_append_panel_row_local` writes `columns=_PANEL_COLUMNS`, which m3-07b grew to 9 by INSERTING `n_dropped_occluded` at index 7 → the TSV gained a 9th column → (b) failed `At index 7 diff`. **DIAGNOSIS CONFIRMED = an OMISSION in the 07a RED, not a deliberate expectation.** Evidence: precedent `1a9d170` (itself quick-260701-qcy) added the PREVIOUS panel column RED-first **AND** updated this SAME assertion in the same commit — its message reads *"updated the panel-TSV column-list assertion for the appended column"*; that companion edit was never made for `n_dropped_occluded`. Per the `do_not`, the column was NOT dropped — dropping it would fail the 07a RED and discard the per-region occlusion provenance `osf.io/az52u` pre-registers. **Blast radius verified contained BEFORE the edit:** `:392` is the ONLY exact-column-list pin for the panel TSV; every other test uses order-independent `set()`/`issubset`; no R/Snakemake/downstream consumer reads the panel TSV at all (only `run_native_ld_panel.py` + its test reference it). **Frozen contracts VERIFIED 0-line diff** (`plink_ld_to_npz.py`, `ld_npz_to_rds.R`, `condition_ld_matrix.py`) and ALL of `src/` untouched since `a76ebe5` — this is a test-only change. | 260715-u22 | **Closed BOTH blast-radius findings from the 2026-07-15 4-agent sweep — two PRE-EXISTING defects, TDD RED-first, one atomic commit each (different gates → independently revertible).** Neither was caused by quick-260715-sqe (`git diff --stat a76ebe5..HEAD -- src/` was EMPTY). **(1) `63bdb59` — the scan→enrich SEAM key contract (BLOCKED m3-07c; silent-wrong-data class).** `enrich_occlusion_manifest` looked up the 07c hand-off by GRCh38 `variant_id` STRING (`occlusion_manifest.py:331`) — and its docstring `:306` DECLARED that wrong contract — while the 07c RED pins the producer to a **(chr, pos) GRCh37 TUPLE** key (`test_occlusion_present_rate_scan.py:79-84`, `target = (1, 5_982_778)`; `variant_id` appears nowhere in that suite). Build 07c exactly to its RED → every `.get(vid, {})` MISSES → **no crash** → `traits_present`/`n_traits_present`/`n_traits_scanned` all `pd.NA` across **all 276 regions**, both suites GREEN, manifest reading "scanned but absent everywhere" (indistinguishable from a real k=0) → would have silently blanked the **rs182965575 "present in 7 of 9 AFR sumstats"** evidence that RETIRED NaN→0, published as the osf.io/az52u provenance. **THE RED WAS RIGHT; THE SHIPPED CONSUMER WAS WRONG** — the scan reads PUBLIC GRCh37 sumstats by CHR/POS and can never compute a GRCh38 variant_id (fixture `SNP_ID` `1:5982778:A:G` vs manifest `variant_id` `1:5922718:A:A` — different coords AND alleles); `out` post-`add_grch37_positions` (`:321`) is the ONLY place carrying both keys, so the join MUST be `(chr, pos_grch37)`, post-lift — a cast cannot fix it. FIX: join on `(chr, pos_grch37)` via a new `_present_rate_key` helper (normalizes numpy int64/float64/str/`chr`-prefix; returns **None** when `pos_grch37` is None/NaN = the variant did not lift, `add_grch37_positions:291`'s explicit signal → correctly `pd.NA`); `:306` docstring corrected to the real contract; **+ a LOUD GUARD** (this bug class is silent-`pd.NA`): if liftable keys exist and NOT ONE matches → **RAISE** with an actionable message naming the required key shape. ⚠ **The plan-checker caught a FALSE-TRIP in the guard I originally specified** — scoping it to `keys` (not `keys_present`) would hard-abort a legitimate region whose occluded variants all sit in a liftover/assembly gap, with a message indistinguishable from a real regression — i.e. it would have traded a silent bug for a loud one that a future maintainer rips out, silently restoring the defect. Corrected to `keys_present = [k for k in keys if k is not None]; if keys_present and not any(...)`, + **Test D** pinning the zero-liftable boundary. 4 new seam tests deliberately MIRROR the sibling guard `test_occlusion_lockstep_drop.py:282` (whose docstring names this exact risk: *"07b and 07c can each ship green while producing a manifest the other cannot consume"*) — **the prior sweep caught ONE seam and missed its sibling in the same module**; the seam test's key `(1, 5_982_778)` is **byte-identical to the 07c RED's `target`**, binding the two suites by IDENTITY not resemblance. `test_occlusion_manifest.py` 11 → **15 passed**. **(2) `fe375e7` — panel-TSV header/arity reconciliation (PRODUCTION PRECONDITION for the ~11-day billed fire; does NOT block merge or 07c).** `_append_panel_row_local` gated the header on file EXISTENCE only (`:481`), read the existing TSV ONLY to dedup `region_id` (`:487`, never comparing `list(existing.columns)` to `_PANEL_COLUMNS`), and appended `header=False` (`:490-493`). REPRODUCED AT $0: seed an 8-col panel + let 9-col code append → `field counts [8, 8, 9]` → the NEXT region's dedup read raises `ParserError: Expected 8 fields in line 3, saw 9`, **UNCAUGHT** (`append_panel_row` is at `:808`, OUTSIDE the per-region try/except; the main loop wraps `process_region` in no try) → **the billed fire ABORTS**. ⚠ **"0/276 banked" does NOT evidence an absent stale TSV** — 0/276 is measured in `.npz`, and the `.npz` (not the panel TSV) gates the resume skip (`:605-608`); the June/July fires DID append error rows unconditionally (`:808`) on 7-col (Jun 28-30) and 8-col (Jul-02 `2d23d67`, `ed9cfd4` verified an ancestor) code. FIX: reconcile the on-disk header against `_PANEL_COLUMNS` after `:487` → RAISE naming both column lists + telling the operator to rotate/delete the stale TSV. Fails loudly at region 1 at ZERO cost instead of corrupting provenance or dying at region 2 after billed compute. NO auto-migration (a guard that silently repairs HIDES the bug — [[feedback_skip_guard_masks_not_fixes]]). `test_run_native_ld_panel.py` 52 → **54 passed**. **HONEST RED-FIRST RECORD (recorded as OBSERVED, not contrived):** Task-1 Tests **A** (`assert nan == 'bmi,ldl'`) and **C** (`DID NOT RAISE`) were the TRUE REDs; Test **B** passes pre-fix *for the wrong reason* (pre-fix NOTHING matches ANY row so everything is NA) and Test **D** passed pre-fix outright — both are post-fix regression guards, and neither was contrived into failing. Task-2 Test A: `DID NOT RAISE`. **VERIFIED:** full `tests/m3` **15 failed / 401 passed / 31 skipped** (baseline 15F/395P/31S → +4 task 1, +2 task 2, **0 regressions**); the 15 REDs UNCHANGED and split exactly **9** `drop_occluded_from_sumstats` + **6** `occlusion_present_rate_scan`. **m3-07c NOT started** (neither module exists; its RED 0-line diff — a DROP below 15 would have meant 07c was built). Frozen contracts 0-line diff; `_PANEL_COLUMNS` unchanged (9 cols, `n_dropped_occluded`@7, monomorphic last); `:392`/`:1281`/`:1590` green. **⚠ P3 NOT ABSORBED — still OPEN:** a transient gsutil blip (`:514-524`, `_gsutil_object_size` bare-excepts to None → mirror absent → fresh 1-row file → unconditional upload OVER the bucket) silently truncates the panel TSV with NO version skew; the `.npz` survive so the fire "completes" with falsified provenance. Needs a code fix; no pre-flight prevents it. Recorded in HANDOFF.json. No perimeter contact; loop NOT re-fired; m3-06 HELD; $0. | 2026-07-15 | 63bdb59, fe375e7 | Done (401P/0 regressions) | [260715-u22-close-blast-radius-findings-scan-enrich-](./quick/260715-u22-close-blast-radius-findings-scan-enrich-/) |

| 260715-vxz | **Closed P3 — the gsutil-blip bucket overwrite that would have SILENTLY destroyed banked panel-TSV provenance mid-fire.** The last CODE gate on the ~11-day billed AoU run; PRE-EXISTING, found by the 2026-07-15 blast-radius sweep and deliberately scoped OUT of quick-260715-u22. **THE DEFECT:** `append_panel_row`'s gs:// mirror-seed had two data-loss paths — **P3a**: `_gsutil_object_size` returned None on ANY gsutil error (bare `except Exception`) → treated as "absent" → no seed → `_append_panel_row_local` took its not-exists() branch → FRESH header + 1 row → `_gsutil_upload` cp'd that 1-row file OVER the bucket object; **P3b (worse)**: stat SUCCEEDED (object exists, size>0) but the seed `cp` FAILED → `except Exception: pass` → same fresh-1-row-then-upload-over, i.e. the code KNEW the object existed and overwrote it anyway. **WHY IT WAS SILENT AND WORSE THAN A CRASH:** the per-region `.npz` (NOT the TSV) gate the resume skip (`:605-608`), so the `.npz` survive, the fire RUNS TO COMPLETION LOOKING HEALTHY, and the loss surfaces only at analysis time. u22's header guard (`fe375e7`) does NOT catch it — the truncated file is written by the CURRENT binary so it stays 9-col, no schema skew, no raise — and NO pre-flight prevents it (it happens mid-run, e.g. day 6 on a VM recycle / token refresh / VPC-SC hiccup). **ROOT CAUSE:** `_gsutil_object_size` had TWO callers with **OPPOSITE failure-safety requirements** — `:209` `_existing_region_npz_gs` (the RESUME GUARD), where a false "absent" → recompute → costs COMPUTE, NO data loss, so its "safer to recompute" docstring is CORRECT and load-bearing for the 276-region skip; vs `:536` the panel seed, where a false "absent" → upload-over → **DATA LOSS**, so "assume absent" is EXACTLY BACKWARDS and the safe default is REFUSE. One helper, two opposite safety requirements. **FIX (`ff8cc47`):** INVARIANT = *never cp the mirror OVER the bucket object unless we POSITIVELY established the mirror contains everything the bucket object contains* (downloaded OK, or definitively absent); INDETERMINATE → RAISE, never guess. New tri-state classifier + a dedicated `PanelBucketStateUnknown(RuntimeError)`: PRESENT → the seed cp MUST succeed (a cp failure now RAISES); DEFINITIVELY-ABSENT → start fresh (unchanged — the legitimate first-region path); INDETERMINATE → RAISE with actionable advice explaining that the loss would be silent and that the TSV is rebuildable from the banked `.npz`. `_GSUTIL_ABSENT_SIGNATURES = ("no urls matched", "no url matched")` is deliberately NARROW + case-insensitive — the ONLY thing that can license an overwrite; 503/403 ServiceException, AccessDenied and FileNotFoundError (gsutil not installed) all stay INDETERMINATE. **NO COLLATERAL:** the fix ADDED a fail-CLOSED helper and left `_gsutil_object_size` (`:186-201`) + `_existing_region_npz_gs` (`:203-217`) **BYTE-IDENTICAL** — PROVEN structurally (the diff's lowest hunk is old line **221** > 217) and behaviorally (test `:814` `stat_error_uris` → `len(mock_plink2.calls) == 1` unmodified + green). ⚠ **THE FALSE-TRIP WAS THE REAL RISK, NOT THE DEFECT:** too-narrow absent-detection ⟹ EVERY legitimate first region raises ⟹ **the entire fire cannot start**. The plan-checker hand-simulated the classifier against 5 inputs (real-plural-absent, mock-singular, 503, 403, FileNotFoundError) and the executor's independently-written probe re-checked control (c) — no misclassification in either direction. **DEFECT DEMONSTRATED POSITIVELY, not inferred:** a probe against live PRE-FIX code showed the bucket going **3 banked rows → 1, silently**, in BOTH P3a and P3b; post-fix the same probe RAISES with **3 → 3 and ZERO cp** to the panel URI. **⚠ ALSO FIXED THE TEST-SEAM LIE THAT LET P3 SURVIVE (same bug class as P3 itself — a test that did not model reality):** `_MockGsutil`'s `cp` was **UPLOAD-ONLY** (`self.objects[dst] = Path(src).stat().st_size` → a download called `Path("gs://…").stat()` → FileNotFoundError), so **the mirror-seed DOWNLOAD path had NEVER been exercised by any test**; and the mock emitted the SINGULAR `"No URL matched"` while real gsutil emits the PLURAL `"No URLs matched: gs://…"`; and its `stat_error_uris` ("simulate an error") raised the **absent** signature, so it could not express INDETERMINATE at all. Mock extended (direction dispatch + contents storage + a genuine indeterminate-error capability + stderr corrected to reality) with **all 6 pre-existing call sites behaviorally unchanged** — all 54 pre-existing tests passed **PRE-fix** against the extended mock = the empirical blast-radius proof (precedent: m3-07b's `_MockPlink` extension). **HONEST RED RECORD:** (a) indeterminate-stat and (b) failed-seed-cp were genuine pre-fix REDs (`DID NOT RAISE`); (c) definitively-absent and (d) happy-path **PASSED pre-fix and were NOT contrived into failing** — (d) only executable at all once the mock was extended. 4 new tests; the data-loss proofs assert **raises AND bucket byte-identical AND no cp** (a `pytest.raises`-only test would be theatre — it passes even if the upload already happened). **VERIFIED:** full `tests/m3` **15 failed / 405 passed / 31 skipped** (401 + 4, **0 regressions**); the 15 REDs unchanged and split exactly **9** `drop_occluded_from_sumstats` + **6** `occlusion_present_rate_scan` ⟹ **m3-07c NOT started** (a drop below 15 would have meant it was). ### Session 2026-05-20 — Wave-1 monolithic completion + iframe-hostile stack-trace verification + workbench env pause

**Background.** Resumed from 2026-05-18 session: AoU Cell 3-7 fired via Run All Below on un-refactored monolithic code at 2026-05-18 03:14:12 UTC against 16× n1-highmem-16 (256 vCPU) Dataproc cluster. Refactor pushed to origin/main as `50f071c` on 2026-05-18 evening.

**Key narrative:**

1. **Multi-day Cell 3-7 monolithic execution** (2026-05-18 03:14 UTC → 2026-05-20 19:08 UTC = ~67h). Three wide compute+write stages observed, each followed by canonical 82-task finalize-cascade (rows component write + metadata.json.gz + _SUCCESS markers):
   - **Stage 36** (7.7h, 4090 tasks) — Cell 3 MT #1 `mt_afr_qc.mt` write (already verified 2026-05-19 via gsutil scrollback)
   - **Stage 45** (9.5h, 4090 tasks) — Cell 4 MT #2 `mt_afr_pca_selfid_qc.mt` write
   - **Stage 62** (16.5h, 4090 tasks) — Cell 5 MT #3 `mt_eur_qc.mt` write
   
   Cell 6 (disjoint-cohort check via `mt.s.collect()` + set arithmetic) likely fired in the ~18s gap between Stage 70 (last MT #3 cascade stage) and Stage 71 (Cell 7's first count_rows).

2. **Cell 7 count_rows velocity collapse** — Stage 71 (Cell 7's `mt_afr_selfid.count_rows()` for the cohort_summary_m3.tsv `n_variants` column) started 2026-05-20 19:08:06 UTC. 4090 tasks at SparkBackend.scala:450. Velocity progression measured across multiple polls:
   - 27 min: 0/4090 (JIT warmup phase, expected)
   - 43 min: 0/4090 (warmup persisting)
   - 2.7h: 252/4090 (window velocity 2.12 t/min — slow but progressing)
   - 3.5h: 265/4090 (window velocity collapsed to 0.27 t/min — 8× deceleration)
   
   Projection at terminal velocity: Stage 71 alone needs ~9-43h more; Stage 72 (mt_eur.count_rows) similar; total $800-$9,000 additional burn. The slow per-task time is structurally inconsistent with Hail's textbook count_rows-on-read-back-MT (which should use cached partition metadata).

3. **Pivot to mid-Cell-7 pause** — Path B1 was selected on 2026-05-19 ~14:30 UTC (cost-equivalent with B2 kill+refactor at MT #2/#3 inflection; iframe-broken; healthy run track record at the time). The Stage 71 velocity collapse changed the math: pause-and-defer-TSV vs continue-grind saved $800-$9,000. The TSV is a 3-row validation-memo summary that Wave 2/3/4 don't require (Cell 8 markdown: "after Wave 2 dev fire signoff, Wave 5 close-out task"). Sample counts derivable from print stdout / refactored re-fire; variant counts derivable from MT rows-component metadata at any future time.

4. **Stack-trace verification (the decisive analysis)** — Carter's correct caution that "if Stage 62 was actually MT #3's write being in-progress (not done), pausing forfeits MT #3" forced rigorous verification under iframe-+terminal-hostility. Methodology:
   - Click `+details` on **Stage 62** (the questioned 16.5h stage) → captured stack trace
   - Click `+details` on **Stage 71** (currently active stage) → captured stack trace
   - Click `+details` on **Stage 36** (KNOWN-GOOD MT #1 write, verified 2026-05-19 via gsutil `_SUCCESS` listing) → captured stack trace as reference
   
   All three stack traces are **structurally identical** modulo JIT class IDs:
   ```
   org.apache.spark.SparkContext.runJob(SparkContext.scala:2446)
   is.hail.backend.spark.SparkBackend.parallelizeAndComputeWithIndex(SparkBackend.scala:449)
   is.hail.backend.BackendUtils.collectDArray(BackendUtils.scala:85)
   __C{N}Compile.__m{M}begin_group_1_region40_91(Emit.scala)
   __C{N}Compile.__m{M}begin_group_1(Emit.scala)
   __C{N}Compile.__m{N+2}split_Block(Emit.scala)
   __C{N}Compile.apply(Emit.scala)
   is.hail.expr.ir.CompileAndEvaluate$.$anonfun$_apply$5(CompileAndEvaluate.scala:83)
   ...
   ```
   
   Since Stage 36 is a **verified successful write**, this stack pattern IS what Hail's write looks like (the write/Parquet-encode/GCS-upload operations are JIT-compiled into the `__C*Compile.__m*` bytecode classes themselves, not exposed as separate stack frames). Therefore Stages 45 and 62 (identical stack) are also writes, and the cascade Stages 46-51 + 63-70 (canonical Hail 82-task-rows-write + per-component _SUCCESS markers) confirm successful commits. **MT #2 + MT #3 strongly inferred committed.**
   
   Additional corroborating evidence: 5 wide+82-task-cascade event pairs observed throughout the run (Stages 10, 19, 36, 45, 62) — three correspond to MT #1/#2/#3 writes; two (Stages 10, 19) likely correspond to source MT reads + naive_coalesce materialization stages in load_qc_cohort.

   Operational pattern memory-worthy for future iframe-broken sessions: stack-trace comparison via Spark UI `+details` clicks is a reliable iframe-free verification path when gsutil is unavailable.

Pre-pull subprocess snippet (literal Python; paste into Untitled2 fresh cell):

```python
import subprocess

script = r"""
set -e

cd ~/coloc_analysis 2>/dev/null \
  || cd ~/repos/coloc_analysis 2>/dev/null \
  || cd /home/jupyter/coloc_analysis 2>/dev/null \
  || { echo "ERROR: clone not found in standard locations"; exit 2; }
echo "--- clone path ---"
pwd

echo ""
echo "--- pre-pull branch + status ---"
echo "branch: $(git branch --show-current)"
echo "status (empty = clean):"
git status --short || true

echo ""
echo "--- pre-pull HEAD ---"
git log --oneline -3

echo ""
echo "--- git pull origin main ---"
git pull origin main

echo ""
echo "--- post-pull HEAD (expect c2f29df / e7063f5 / 5389a88 / 36e8062 visible) ---"
git log --oneline -5

echo ""
echo "--- template structure check ---"
python3 << 'PYEOF'
import json
n = json.load(open('.planning/notebooks/AOU-1_template.ipynb'))
print(f'total cells: {len(n["cells"])}')
for i, c in enumerate(n['cells']):
    src = c['source']
    head = (src[0] if isinstance(src, list) and src else str(src))[:75].replace('\n', ' ')
    print(f'  cell {i}: type={c["cell_type"]:8s} head={head!r}')
PYEOF

echo ""
echo "--- workspace-root AOU-1 location ---"
ls -la /home/jupyter/AOU-1_cohort_definition.ipynb 2>/dev/null \
  || find /home/jupyter -maxdepth 3 -name 'AOU-1_cohort_definition.ipynb' 2>/dev/null \
  || echo "(workspace-root file not found in /home/jupyter; will write fresh)"
"""

result = subprocess.run(['bash'], input=script, capture_output=True, text=True)
print(result.stdout)
if result.stderr:
    print("--- stderr ---")
    print(result.stderr)
print(f"--- exit code: {result.returncode} ---")
```

| # | Bug | Root Cause | Fix | Commit |

### Earlier in this session (2026-04-14 PM)

### Finding 2026-04-14 PM — Phase 0 idempotency gap

Dry-run inspection of Phase A narrow-scout targets revealed:

- `download_ldsc_baseline` rule (src/snakemake/rules/pathway.smk:180-237) re-fetches 4 large tarballs (~5 GB total) from `broad-alkesgroup-ukbb-ld.s3.amazonaws.com` + GCS `requester-pays` paths. Manually-staged data IS on disk (`data/reference/ldsc/baselineLD.{1..22}.{annot.gz,l2.M,l2.M_5_50}`, `data/reference/ldsc/eur_w_ld_chr/w_hm3.snplist`, `data/reference/ldsc/w_hm3.snplist`) but the rule's flag file `data/reference/ldsc/.baseline_download_done` doesn't exist, so Snakemake re-runs.
- `download_magma_binary` rule expects `tools/magma_v1.10/magma` which doesn't exist; Carter's manual download landed at `data/reference/magma/magma`. Path mismatch + JS-gated CNCR upstream.
- `harmonize_sumstats` chains `download_sumstats` for 8 trait/ancestry combos (bmi.EUR, t2d.EUR, t2d.AFR, hypertension.EUR, asthma.EUR, asthma.AFR, stroke.EUR, stroke.AFR). Raw sumstats not on disk. Multiple URL-rot risk.
- Existing harmonized .bgz files (Feb 11 timestamps, ~2.3 GB at `data/processed/region_analysis/sumstats_harmonized_fixed/`) would be overwritten because `config/datasets.yaml` mtime is newer.

**Decision:** No real-data execution until Phase 0 download rules are made idempotent (skip-if-on-disk semantics) AND the magma binary path is reconciled. Routed to `/gsd-quick`.
